from google.colab import files
import zipfile
import os
# This opens the 'Choose File' dialog
uploaded = files.upload()
# Get the name of the file you just selected
zip_name = list(uploaded.keys())[0]
# Extract it immediately
extract_path = './alzheimer_data'
with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_name, 'r') as zip_ref:
    zip_ref.extractall(extract_path)
print(f"✅ Successfully selected and extracted: {zip_name}")

spark Gemini 
import zipfile
import os

# 1. Extract the uploaded zip
with zipfile.ZipFile('archive.zip', 'r') as zip_ref:
    zip_ref.extractall('./alzheimer_dataset')

# Debugging step: Print contents of the extracted directory
print("Contents of './alzheimer_dataset':")
extracted_contents = os.listdir('./alzheimer_dataset')
print(extracted_contents)

# 2. Automatically find the training directory
train_dir = ""
test_dir = "" # Initialize test_dir

# Based on the `!ls -R` output, the categories (MildDemented, NonDemented, etc.)
# are directly inside './alzheimer_dataset/combined_images'.
# So, 'combined_images' acts as the root for our training data.
# We assume a similar structure for test data if it exists, but the ls -R did not show a separate test folder.

if 'combined_images' in extracted_contents:
    potential_data_root = os.path.join('./alzheimer_dataset', 'combined_images')
    # Check if this folder contains the category folders directly
    categories = [d for d in os.listdir(potential_data_root) if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(potential_data_root, d))]
    if categories: # If there are subdirectories (categories), this is our data root
        train_dir = potential_data_root
        # For now, we'll set test_dir to the same path if no explicit test set is found
        # The user might need to specify a separate test_dir if it exists elsewhere.
        test_dir = potential_data_root # Assuming combined_images serves as both train and test root for now
    else:
        # Fallback if 'combined_images' itself doesn't contain categories
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk('./alzheimer_dataset'):
            if any(d in ['MildDemented', 'ModerateDemented', 'NonDemented', 'VeryMildDemented'] for d in dirs):
                train_dir = root
                test_dir = root # Placeholder
                break
else:
    # Original logic if 'combined_images' is not the immediate subfolder
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk('./alzheimer_dataset'):
        if 'train' in dirs and 'test' in dirs:
            train_dir = os.path.join(root, 'train')
            test_dir = os.path.join(root, 'test')
            break

if train_dir:
    print(f"✅ Data root (train_dir) set to: {train_dir}")
    print("Stages found (subfolders):", [d for d in os.listdir(train_dir) if os.path.isdir(os.path.join(train_dir, d))])
    if train_dir == test_dir:
        print("Note: No separate 'test' directory found; assuming combined data for now.")
else:
    print("❌ Could not automatically find relevant data directories.")
    print("Please inspect the contents of './alzheimer_dataset' and manually specify the `train_dir` and `test_dir`.")

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import cv2
import random

categories = os.listdir(train_dir)
plt.figure(figsize=(15, 10))

for i, category in enumerate(categories):
    folder_path = os.path.join(train_dir, category)
    img_name = random.choice(os.listdir(folder_path))
    img_path = os.path.join(folder_path, img_name)

    # Load image
    img = cv2.imread(img_path)
    img = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)

    plt.subplot(1, 4, i+1)
    plt.imshow(img)
    plt.title(f"Stage: {category}")
    plt.axis('off')

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import cv2
import random

categories = os.listdir(train_dir)
plt.figure(figsize=(15, 10))

for i, category in enumerate(categories):
    folder_path = os.path.join(train_dir, category)
    img_name = random.choice(os.listdir(folder_path))
    img_path = os.path.join(folder_path, img_name)

    # Load image
    img = cv2.imread(img_path)
    img = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)

    plt.subplot(1, 4, i+1)
    plt.imshow(img)
    plt.title(f"Stage: {category}")
    plt.axis('off')

plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

from tensorflow.keras.preprocessing.image import ImageDataGenerator

# Standardize image size
IMG_HEIGHT, IMG_WIDTH = 176, 176
BATCH_SIZE = 32

train_datagen = ImageDataGenerator(
    rescale=1./255,
    rotation_range=10,
    zoom_range=0.1,
    fill_mode='nearest',
    validation_split=0.2 # Using 20% of train data for internal validation
)

train_generator = train_datagen.flow_from_directory(
    train_dir,
    target_size=(IMG_HEIGHT, IMG_WIDTH),
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    class_mode='categorical',
    subset='training'
)

val_generator = train_datagen.flow_from_directory(
    train_dir,
    target_size=(IMG_HEIGHT, IMG_WIDTH),
    batch_size=BATCH_SIZE,
    class_mode='categorical',
    subset='validation'
)

import tensorflow as tf
from tensorflow.keras.applications import ResNet50
from tensorflow.keras import layers, models

def build_alzheimer_model():
    # Load ResNet50 without the top classification layer
    base_model = ResNet50(weights='imagenet', include_top=False, input_shape=(176, 176, 3))

    # Freeze the base model layers so we don't destroy the pre-trained weights
    base_model.trainable = False

    model = models.Sequential([
        base_model,
        layers.GlobalAveragePooling2D(),
        layers.Dense(256, activation='relu'),
        layers.Dropout(0.3),  # Helps the model generalize better
        layers.Dense(4, activation='softmax') # 4 classes: Mild, Moderate, Non, Very Mild
    ])

    model.compile(
        optimizer='adam',
        loss='categorical_crossentropy',
        metrics=['accuracy', tf.keras.metrics.AUC(name='auc')]
    )
    return model

model = build_alzheimer_model()
model.summary()

from tensorflow.keras.applications import ResNet50
from tensorflow.keras import layers, models
def build_alzheimer_model():
    # Load ResNet50 without the top classification layer
    base_model = ResNet50(weights='imagenet', include_top=False, input_shape=(176, 176, 3))
    # Freeze the base model layers so we don't destroy the pre-trained weights
    base_model.trainable = False
    model = models.Sequential([
        base_model,
        layers.GlobalAveragePooling2D(),
        layers.Dense(256, activation='relu'),
        layers.Dropout(0.3),  # Helps the model generalize better
        layers.Dense(4, activation='softmax') # 4 classes: Mild, Moderate, Non, Very Mild
    ])
    model.compile(
        optimizer='adam',
        loss='categorical_crossentropy',
        metrics=['accuracy', tf.keras.metrics.AUC(name='auc')]
    )
    return model
model = build_alzheimer_model()
model.summary()

from tensorflow.keras.applications import ResNet50
from tensorflow.keras import layers, models
def build_alzheimer_model():
    # Load ResNet50 without the top classification layer
    base_model = ResNet50(weights='imagenet', include_top=False, input_shape=(176, 176, 3))
    # Freeze the base model layers so we don't destroy the pre-trained weights
    base_model.trainable = False
    model = models.Sequential([
        base_model,
        layers.GlobalAveragePooling2D(),
        layers.Dense(256, activation='relu'),
        layers.Dropout(0.3),  # Helps the model generalize better
        layers.Dense(4, activation='softmax') # 4 classes: Mild, Moderate, Non, Very Mild
    ])
    model.compile(
        optimizer='adam',
        loss='categorical_crossentropy',
        metrics=['accuracy', tf.keras.metrics.AUC(name='auc')]
    )
    return model
model = build_alzheimer_model()
model.summary()

    
 
 

        

          

          

            
 

          

        

      

    

      spark
      Gemini
    

      

        

          
          

            
          

        

      
from tensorflow.keras.callbacks import EarlyStopping, ReduceLROnPlateau
early_stopping = EarlyStopping(monitor='val_loss', patience=5, restore_best_weights=True)
reduce_lr = ReduceLROnPlateau(monitor='val_loss', factor=0.2, patience=3, min_lr=0.00001)
# Start the training sprint
history = model.fit(
    train_generator,
    validation_data=val_generator,
    epochs=15, # Good for a one-day project
    callbacks=[early_stopping, reduce_lr]
)
